## A.5 The RNG algorithm (`rng.ts`) — for faithful ports

```
A = 1103515245, C = 12345, M = 2^31
nextSeed(s)      = (s*A + C) mod M          # 62-bit product; only the low 31 bits matter (M = 2^31)
bits(s')         = floor(s' / 32768) mod 65536   # upper bits 15..30
rollDie(s)       -> s' = nextSeed(s); value = min(5, floor(bits(s')/10923)) + 1   # 1..6
randBelow(s, n)  -> n<=0 ? {s,0} : (s'=nextSeed(s); {s', bits(s') mod n})
shuffle(s, arr)  -> Fisher–Yates, i = len-1 .. 1, j = randBelow(i+1), swap; returns {advanced seed, new array}
```

> Port note: because `M = 2^31` is a power of two, only the low 31 bits of the product are needed — a 32-bit truncating unsigned multiply masked with `& 0x7FFFFFFF` reproduces `nextSeed` exactly; 64-bit math is not required.

## A.6 Full state shape (`state.ts`) — port-grade field inventory

The complete `GameState` and its nested records, verbatim from `state.ts:33-223`. Optional (`?`)
fields may be absent — a port should treat absent as the stated default. All values are integers,
booleans, or arrays thereof; nothing requires floating point.

### GameState (state.ts:109-223)

| Field | Type | Meaning / default |
|-------|------|-------------------|
| gs | int | GS_PLAYING 0 / ESCAPED 1 / DEAD 2 / QUIT 3 (SC-3-22) |
| phase | enum | `explore \| medusa \| encounter \| fight \| pickup \| gameOver` (SC-4-3) |
| turn | int | starts 1; +1 per successful move (SC-4-9) |
| score | int | running score field (final score is computed by §12 `scoreBreakdown`) |
| curses | int | accumulated curses (Eye of God, Chest curse roll) |
| bonusScore | int | banked Chest loot added at scoring (SC-12) |
| sorcererKilled | bool | lifts the curse penalty (§12) |
| areas | PlacedArea[] | placed map, index 0 = the Gateway |
| partyArea | int | index into `areas` |
| level | int | current cave level, starts 1 |
| prev / prev2 | int | area index one / two moves back (withdraw target / earthquake) |
| party | PartyMember[] | the party, in join order |
| largePack / largeIdx | int[] / int | shuffled 60 area-card values; next-draw cursor (SC-5-10/12) |
| smallPack / smallIdx | int[] / int | shuffled 71-card codes minus party picks; cursor (SC-5-11/12, SC-5-5) |
| strangers | int[] | chamber working set: creature ids present (SC-7.1-5) |
| treasures | int[] | working set: treasure ids on the live floor |
| hazards | int[] | working set: hazard ids drawn this entry (cleared after firing, SC-7.2-10) |
| sleeping? | int[] | Lotus-slept creature ids — inert; default `[]` |
| lulled? | int[] | flute-lulled Dragons, recomputed per entry (SC-7.1-7); default `[]` |
| seed | int | the LCG cursor (A.5) — the ONLY randomness state (SC-5-13) |
| fight | FightState \| null | active fight, else null |
| fellThroughTrap? | bool | position reached by one-way trap fall; blocks withdraw/retreat (SC-4-14/17); default false |
| surpriseReady? | bool | fresh chamber entry, attack-now grants surprise (SC-4-16); default false |
| secretDoors? | int | count of secret doors discovered (labels A, B, C…, SC-6.1-13); default 0 |
| lotusOnSorcerer? | bool | Sorcerer weakened −2 FS (can't be slept); default false |
| medusaPause? | {freshEntry, extraDraw?} | set while phase="medusa": entry held pre-hazards for the Lotus decision (SC-7.2-14); absent otherwise. `extraDraw?: {hadSurprise?}` (kit-only, SC-EXT-7/8) is set instead of a plain `{freshEntry:false}` when the pause was opened by a Well/Bell draw into an already-entered chamber — carries the pre-draw `surpriseReady` through to the resumed tail so it restores rather than being clobbered by the default recompute, and forces the Dragon-lull notice regardless of chamber freshness (SC-4-16) |
| indiffStreak? | int | live working copy of this area's indifference count, restored from `indiffCounts` on every chamber entry (3 ⇒ pacify, SC-4-20, SC-8.4-5); default 0 |
| indiffCounts? | Record\<int,int\> | DURABLE, per-area indifference count, keyed by area index — persists across visits (bug fix 2026-08-04, SC-8.4-5); default absent/`{}` = none tested yet |
| indiffLeaveOpen? | bool | one-shot "may leave by any doorway" window, opened by a qualifying indifferent test, closed by a dead-end leave attempt or a fresh test/entry (SC-4-18a, bug fix 2026-08-04); default false |
| pacifiedAreas? | int[] | area indices permanently indifferent to this party; default `[]` |
| unicornGuardAreas? | int[] | SUBSET of `pacifiedAreas` pacified by the womanless-Unicorn-guard case rather than indifference (SC-EXT-19, kit-only); excludes the Thief's pickup-unlock even though it shares `pacifiedAreas`; default absent/`[]` = none |
| hostileAreas? | int[] | areas retreated from — attack on sight on return (SC-4-28); default `[]` |
| fightDrops? | {mi,tid}[] | heavy treasure dropped for THIS fight, with its dropper (SC-7.3-10); default `[]` |
| variants? | {extensionKit?} | solo game variants fixed at `newGame`, immutable thereafter (SC-EXT-1); default absent = today's behaviour |
| noWithdrawTurn? | int | turn number `withdraw` is blocked for — a Well draw or Bell Rope 4-6 roll (SC-EXT-9, kit-only); default absent = never blocked this way |
| statues? | int[] | Gallery's inert stone-creature working set (SC-EXT-10, kit-only); reset to `[]` ONLY on a Gallery entry — stays undefined for every other chamber, so it never appears in a kit-off game's state; default absent |
| lairCoord? | int | packed coord of the Lair once placed AND entered (SC-EXT-12, kit-only); default absent = not yet found |
| harpyStash? | int[] | treasure ids stolen by Harpies pending the Lair's discovery (SC-EXT-12, kit-only); default absent/`[]` = nothing pending |
| cryptCoord? | int | packed coord of an unresolved, parked Crypt (SC-EXT-13, kit-only); cleared the instant `enterCrypt` resolves it; default absent = none parked (or already resolved) |
| thiefPickup? | bool | a Thief-unlocked pickup session over indifference-pacified guarded treasure (SC-EXT-19, kit-only); `takeTreasure` narrates each lift while set; cleared (`delete`) the moment `persistAndExplore` next runs; default absent = an ordinary pickup |
| subLocation? | {area,at:"island"} | Precise Locations (SC-10.5-5/7): explicit `jumpToIsland` override for a same-tile sub-location shift geometry can't derive; valid only while `area === partyArea` — cleared on every real move (relocateDown/carpetMove/the "move" case); default absent = derive from position (`subLocation.ts`'s `getSubLocation`) |
| pendingDrops? | Record\<int,int[]\> | Special-areas revision (SC-10.5-16, bug fix 2026-08-08): a deliberate Chasm/Whirlpool drop queued for the level below, keyed by that level's packed target coord; delivered (and its entry deleted) the moment ANY party genuinely enters that coordinate. Unlike `harpyStash`/`lairCoord` (a per-party ledger, SC-EXT-12), this is treated as CAVE-SHARED in multiplayer (`multi.ts`'s `CaveState.pendingDrops`, SC-10.5-12) — a fact about the shared map, not a private bookkeeping entry, mirroring `sunkTreasure`'s own precedent; default absent/`{}` = nothing pending |
| `testMode?` | `true` | Test Mode: set once at `newGame`, never toggled. Gates the four `test*` actions. |
| `testNextArea?` | `{dir, special}` | Test Mode: armed next-area override, consumed by `tryMove`. |
| `testNextChamber?` | `{strangers, treasures, hazards}` | Test Mode: armed next-chamber override, consumed by `enterChamber`. |
| `testNextReaction?` | `"friendly"\|"indifferent"\|"hostile"` | Test Mode: armed reaction override, consumed by the `test` action. |

### PartyMember (state.ts:33-58)

| Field | Type | Meaning / default |
|-------|------|-------------------|
| creatureId | int | index into the A.1 table |
| status | int | 0 original / 1 ally / 2 stone / 3 dead (SC-3-21) |
| dragonKills | int | >0 doubles the member's creature points (§12) |
| treasure | int[] | carried treasure ids |
| borne? | int[] | subset of `treasure` being borne — only ids 3/9/10/17/20 (SC-7.3-13, SC-EXT-26/27); default `[]` |
| potionActive? | bool | Strength Potion active this fight (+2 FS); default false |
| stoneArea? | int | where Medusa struck (status 2 only) |
| mpTag? | string | multiplayer-only union identity tag (SC-MP-32); solo never sets it |

### PlacedArea (state.ts:60-81)

| Field | Type | Meaning / default |
|-------|------|-------------------|
| card | int | area-card value (bit-field, SC-3-2) |
| coord | int | packed `level*10000 + y*100 + x` (SC-3-19) |
| faceUp | bool | entered vs dead-end face-down placement (SC-6.1-8/9) |
| visited | bool | chamber contents already drawn once (SC-7.1-2/6) |
| contents | int[] | parked codes 100+cid / 200+tid / 300+hid / 400+cid / 500+cid (SC-3-17; 500+cid is a Gallery statue, SC-EXT-10, kit-only) |
| flags | int | AF_DESTROYED=4 earthquake collapse (SC-7.2-2); AF_BELL_SPENT=8 Bell Rope pulled (SC-EXT-8, kit-only); AF_UNRESOLVED=16 a Spell-remapped card not yet stepped on — face-down to the renderer, cleared on the next real `resolveArea` (SC-EXT-28, kit-only) |
| indiffCount | int | reserved AI counter (persisted; solo play leaves it 0) |
| dropped? | int[] | Deep-Pool sunken heavy treasure ids, automatic on a non-Giant crossing, un-bucketed (SC-10.2); default `[]` |
| sunkTreasure? | {at,items[]}[] | Precise Locations (SC-10.5-9): a DELIBERATE `dropTreasure` cast onto one of the four specials, bucketed by sub-location — `at` is `island` or a doorway direction (DIR_N..DIR_W, 1-4); distinct from `dropped` above; default absent/`[]` = none sunk |
| markers? | int[] | display-only hazard scars (300+hid), never re-fire (SC-7.2-10); default `[]` |
| mirroredStairs? | int | connectivity-only stair bits 32/64, excluded from art (SC-6.1-13..17); default 0 |
| secretDoor? | int | 0-based discovery ordinal of this secret door; absent = none |
| medusaAsleepUntil? | int | Lotus-slept Medusa: no gaze while `turn <=` this; cleared when she wakes (SC-7.2-15); absent = awake |

### FightState / BattlePlan (state.ts:84-107)

| Field | Type | Meaning / default |
|-------|------|-------------------|
| surprise | int | +1 party / −1 strangers / 0 — round 1 only (SC-9.2) |
| round | int | starts 1; retreat legal only after round 1 (SC-4-27) |
| focus | int | index into `strangers` |
| casualtyQueue? | int[][] | pending losing pairs; player picks who falls (SC-4-26) |
| retreatBlocked? | bool | a dead-end retreat this round; must fight on (SC-4-27) |
| PlanMatch | {front[],backers[],strangers[]} | one pairing of a battle plan (SC-9.1-1) |

## A.7 Extension-kit data tables — pinned by `kit-data.test.ts`

All ids below append strictly after the base ranges (A.1–A.3). They live in tables SEPARATE from
`CREATURES`/`TREASURES`/`HAZARD_NAMES` — `KIT_CREATURES`, `KIT_TREASURES`, `KIT_HAZARD_NAMES` — so
those base tables keep the exact shape `data.test.ts` pins (unmodified, still green); `ALL_CREATURES`/
`ALL_TREASURES`/`ALL_HAZARD_NAMES` concatenate base+kit for id-indexed lookup (SC-EXT-2).

| 15 | Demon | 0 | 6 | 0 | null | 0 | 16 = INHUMAN | 6 | 6 | 10 | never joins a chamber — materializes into `prev` instead (US-13, SC-EXT-21); always hostile; fightable only by magic or a Magic Axe bearer; never selectable |

**Kit-on party selection:** `KIT_STARTING_STOCK` = Lion 1, Scholar 1, Witch 3, Thief 1, Wolf 1, plus

### Deck composition (`decks.ts`, `data/smallPack.ts` — SC-EXT-4)

`smallPackExtension()` is 30 codes: 11 creatures (Apprentice, Demon, Lion, Scholar, Witch×3, Thief,
Wolf, +1 Dwarf, +1 Woman), 15 treasures (Gold×3, Silver×3, Gems, Lotus Dust, Elixir, Holy Water,
Magic Axe, Idol, Scroll, Magic Shield, Crypt/Gems), 4 hazards (Desertion, Harpies, Quarrel, Spell) —
concatenated onto the 71-card `smallPackTemplate()` before the shuffle when `variants.extensionKit`
is set, for a 101-card small pack. `EXT_AREA_CARDS`'s 30 tiles are concatenated onto the 60-value
large-pack template the same way, for a 90-card area deck. Kit off ⇒ both builders shuffle exactly
the pre-kit templates, byte-identical to before this milestone.

# Appendix B — Corrections vs the v1 design-spec

The v1 `design-spec.html` predates most of the artifact, hazard, combat, and multiplayer work; its §15 "Gap Analysis" and §16 "Implementation Sketches for the Open Gaps" are almost entirely obsolete. Key corrections (code is authoritative):

**Data model (§3/§5):** the small pack is **71 cards** (one finite deck shared between party selection and chamber draws), not the old two-pool 52-card model; hazards ship **two Earthquakes** (not one); area-card index 41 is **42 (EWU)**, not 74; the seed is a **caller-supplied parameter**, not clock-derived.

**Turn model (§2.2/§4):** the engine is a **pure phase-machine reducer** (`reduce(state, action) → {state, events}`), not the old blocking keystroke loop; the five `phase` values and the full 16-action / 47-event catalogs are the real boundary and were absent from v1.

**Movement (§6):** earthquake-rubble impassability, ascent secret doors + numbered markers, the **never-rotate** tile rule, and length-driven pack-exhaustion are all real and were missing/incorrect in v1.

**Encounters (§8):** the human reaction tables are **real** (Hero 3/3, Priest 1/4, Man/Woman 2/4, Dwarf 0/4 — not em-dash); the **Dragon is 4/6** (1–4 hostile), not always-hostile; Attack is **+1 only on a fresh entry** (not unconditional); Withdraw is **not always available**; the womanless-Unicorn guard, Charmed-Flute Dragon lull, and pacified-chamber Attack are all implemented.

**Fights (§9):** combat is a **submitted battle plan** with a full validation set (two-vs-two ban, must-engage-all, gang-up, enemy-caster fold-in), not auto focus-fire; Ring invincibility, Strength Potion activation, casters-fight-at-total-strength, heavy-treasure drop, blocked-retreat, and the choose-a-casualty die are all implemented.

**Special areas (§10):** the Viper-Pit fatal threshold is **1 or 2**; Deep-Pool's AUTOMATIC crossing drop still sinks to one un-bucketed `dropped` list on the tile (no per-doorway model) — but as of Precise Locations (SC-10.5, 2026-07-30) a DELIBERATE `dropTreasure` cast onto the Deep Pool or Viper Pit now *does* sink precisely into the sub-location it was cast from (`PlacedArea.sunkTreasure`), and Viper-Pit sunk treasure is now Flute-recoverable for that voluntary case; flute-based recovery of a FALLEN member's treasure (lost outright to the pit, `m.treasure=[]`) remains **not** modelled (§10.1-3) — a deliberately unchanged, separate gap. The Chasm and Whirlpool were briefly given the same no-creature-gate sink but, per the special-areas revision (bug fix 2026-08-08), a deliberate drop there now falls through to the level below instead (SC-10.5-16), not into `sunkTreasure` at all.

**Artifacts (§11):** every "not implemented" in the v1 status table (Talisman, Healing Balm, Lotus Dust, Magic Carpet, Lost Ruby statue, Eye of God, Treasure Chest, Magic Staff reanimation) and every §16 sketch is now **shipped code**; the Lost-Ruby statue strikes **only the wrestler** (it does not stay aroused); the Eye curses on drop, transfer, **and** death.

**Scoring (§12):** the curse penalty is a **flat −30** (not −30 per curse) and is lifted entirely by slaying the Sorcerer; the formula now includes **`bonusScore`** (banked Chest loot); "no breakdown on quit" is a UI decision, not an engine rule (the engine only zeroes for `GS_DEAD`).

**Multiplayer (§MP):** entirely undocumented in v1 (which listed multiplayer as out of scope); now implemented in `multi.ts` as a shared-cave / per-seat-party model, with the M3–M7 interaction layer (trades, PvP, unions, concurrency, variants) in `multi-*.ts`. Deliberate omissions/simplifications in that layer (each documented at its module header): `refuseMove` is implemented as ≡ `leaveUnion` (refusing the commander's move IS leaving); rear-guard detachments deter absolutely — `hostileDetachmentAt` blocks a rival's pickup but no guard auto-defence fight is fought (TODO M6+); a disagreed recruit allocation parks the ally as an ordinary neutral stranger rather than awaiting the ensuing fight's victor; zombie corpse ABSORPTION and the one-turn re-animation of a PvP-slain zombie party are not modelled (no per-tile corpse model — a risen party slain again is terminal for good); and borne items rest with the flesh — they are LOST when a wiped party rises as zombies.

# Appendix C — Test-coverage gaps

**None.** Every requirement in Part I now carries a direct engine unit test (see its `test` column). The gaps this appendix previously tracked were closed by dedicated characterization suites under `packages/engine/src/`:

`gap-data-rng`, `gap-contract`, `gap-movement`, `gap-encounters`, `gap-fights`, `gap-special`, `gap-artifacts`, `gap-scoring`, `gap-multiplayer`, and `gap-misc` (`gap-*.test.ts`).

The borne/carried death-loot model (SC-7.3-13/14, SC-7.2-13, SC-9.5-11) is pinned by `loot.test.ts`. In addition, `solo-golden.test.ts` is the **solo golden firewall**: deterministic policy-bot playthroughs whose full replays are snapshotted, freezing solo behaviour — any engine change that alters a solo game's course fails the snapshot and must be a deliberate, reviewed update. `kit-golden.test.ts` (Task 17) is its kit-on counterpart: one `variants:{extensionKit:true}` playthrough (seed 165018, party 18/20 — Witch, Wolf; originally seed 397, re-swept 2026-08-02 when the Chasm stopped drawing a small-pack card on arrival), driven by a kit-biased policy chosen by a scratchpad seed sweep to exercise all four kit special areas, all four kit hazards, a kit artifact use, and multiple kit-creature reactions in one run; it freezes kit-on behaviour the same way, and leaves `solo-golden.test.ts`'s own fixtures untouched. `multi-kit-golden.test.ts` (task 9) completes the set as the MULTIPLAYER kit firewall: one `variants:{extensionKit:true}` two-seat game (seed 7660 — originally seed 215, re-swept to seed 93917 bug fix 2026-08-03 alongside `kit-golden.test.ts`'s own reseed for the Whirlpool/Well/Bell-Rope fix, then re-swept AGAIN to seed 7660 bug fix 2026-08-04 for the move-after-indifferent fix (SC-4-18a), which widened the kit-biased policy's legal-move pool at whatever indifferent-streak encounter seed 93917's playthrough passed through and stopped it reaching a natural finish at all — seat 0 Ogre/Dwarf, seat 1 Man/Scholar — a kit-gated draft, SC-EXT-36) driven through `mpReduce` by the same kit-biased policy around a scripted union beat (formed on the Gateway before the first move, dissolved on the commander's first at-rest turn from step 40 onward), snapshotting the per-seat action/event narrative and a hash of the whole final `MpGameState`. Its describe block is `MP-kit golden firewall — kit-on multiplayer behaviour is frozen`, and its four tests are › `seed 7660 kit-on 2-seat game (union → dissolve) plays back identically`; › `SC-EXT-34: the union Quarrel crosses owners, and the loaned casualty goes home dead at the dissolve`; › `kit-off: the same scripted 2-seat game is byte-identical for absent / {} / {extensionKit:false}`; › `kit-off: the golden's own draft is rejected — the fixture is kit-gated end to end`. The reseed was chosen by a fresh ~20,000-seed sweep filtered to REQUIRE the same genuine cross-seat Quarrel pairing the fixture's headline depends on, so no downstream identity assertion needed a logical change, only step-number updates; the run still pins ≥2 kit hazards (Quarrel, Spell, Harpies-strikes), ≥1 kit special area beyond the minimum (Whirlpool entered+crossed, Crypt parked+entered, Chasm descended), a kit artifact use (the Scroll), and a kit creature (the Demon) fought through the composed MP path (SC-EXT-31) — ending in a natural double wipe (seat 1 first, then seat 0), not a step-cap truncation. The milestone's headline beat is asserted in the OPEN rather than left to the snapshot's state fingerprint, since the narrative lines carry event types without payloads: the second test names the union Quarrel's own `{aId, bId, loserId}` at the step it fires (the commander's own Ogre against seat 1's loaned Man, the loan the side that falls) and the returned corpse's `{creatureId, status: 3, mpTag: undefined}` in its OWNER's roster after the dissolve — so an SC-EXT-34 regression fails with a legible diff instead of an 8-hex hash change. It touches neither `solo-golden.test.ts`'s nor `kit-golden.test.ts`'s fixtures. Two port-support suites extend the same idea: `data/data.test.ts › creature table matches engine-spec Appendix A.1 verbatim` pins every cell of the A.1 table, and `conformance-vectors.test.ts` regenerates and guards the committed Appendix D vector files as file snapshots (regenerate deliberately with `vitest run -u`) — its own `-escape` fixture was reseeded twice for the same SC-4-18a work: seed 225 → 1013 (bug fix 2026-08-04, round 1) after the original stopped reaching an escape ending under the initial move-after-indifferent fix, then 1013 → 11590 (bug fix 2026-08-04, round 2) once `withdraw` was fixed to resolve its destination properly (Gap A) — seed 1013's own playthrough withdrew into exactly the kind of abandoned encounter that fix now reopens, cascading into a full reshuffle that ended in death instead of an escape.

Full engine suite: **917 tests green** (the Magic Carpet's no-secret-door-back fix, SC-11-43, and its "left behind, not destroyed" fix, SC-11-44, are both pinned by `carpet.test.ts` (with SC-11-44's retrieval also cross-checked by gap-contract.test.ts's own SC-4-40 case); Holy Water's "wake a PARKED Gallery statue" fix (SC-EXT-39) is pinned by `kit-holywater-scroll.test.ts`, with its web-side target-name decoding pinned by `holyWaterLabel.test.ts` (apps/web, 530 tests); Precise Locations — the sub-location model, jumpToIsland,
adjacency-gated crossing, and precise dropped treasure across all four specials (SC-10.5), including
the special-areas revision's pending-drop fall-through for the Chasm/Whirlpool (SC-10.5-16) and its
cave-sharing (SC-10.5-12) — is
pinned solo by `precise-locations.test.ts` and its free multiplayer inheritance by
`precise-locations-multi.test.ts`; the Whirlpool's stairway block and trap/descent skip-past
(SC-EXT-6, special-areas revision) is pinned by `kit-descents.test.ts`; the M3–M7 interaction layer is pinned by `multi-trade.test.ts`, `multi-fight.test.ts`, `multi-union.test.ts`, `multi-concurrent.test.ts` and `multi-zombies.test.ts`, whose own zombie×kit-hazard classification (SC-EXT-33, task 4) is pinned alongside it; the union-scoped kit rules — Quarrel across the combined force, a loaned casualty staying on loan, and Desertion/Bell-vanish ending a loan cleanly (SC-EXT-34, task 5) — by `multi-union.test.ts`, and the Magic Shield's PvP pairing ward plus Elixir/Eye parity in PvP totals (SC-EXT-35, task 5) by `multi-fight.test.ts`; the M8 multiplayer extension-kit variant threading (deck widening + `compose` pass-through), kit-creature PvP lookups, shared kit content across seats (Harpy/Lair, Demon, Gallery, Whirlpool, Well/Bell, Crypt — SC-EXT-32), and the draft's own variant-aware `validatePicks` threading (SC-EXT-36, task 7b) are pinned by `multi-kit.test.ts`, with the whole multiplayer×kit combination frozen end to end by `multi-kit-golden.test.ts` (task 9, above); the extension kit's variant plumbing and data tables are pinned by `kit-variant.test.ts` and `kit-data.test.ts`; its Chasm/Whirlpool special-area rules by `kit-descents.test.ts`; its Well/Bell Rope rules by `kit-well-bell.test.ts`; its Gallery/Lair rules by `kit-lair-gallery.test.ts`; its Crypt/Desertion rules by `kit-crypt-desertion.test.ts`; its Harpies/Quarrel/Spell rules by `kit-harpies-quarrel-spell.test.ts`; its class-based artifact eligibility, Wolf's remaining immunities, and the Thief's pickup unlock by `kit-creatures.test.ts`; its Apprentice/Demon rules by `kit-apprentice-demon.test.ts`; its Elixir/Idol rules by `kit-elixir-idol.test.ts`; its Holy Water/Scroll rules by `kit-holywater-scroll.test.ts`; its Magic Axe/Magic Shield rules by `kit-axe-shield.test.ts`; its kit-on party-selection validation and small-pack removal by `kit-selection.test.ts`, plus `replay.test.ts`'s own variants-threading cases; its kit-on golden regression firewall by `kit-golden.test.ts`; Test Mode (§Test Mode) — testMode gating, the three armed overrides (each independently defended against a hand-crafted, non-test state), and their consumption by tryMove/enterChamber/the test action — is pinned by test-mode.test.ts; the bug fix 2026-08-09 (QOTO-01) correction — a Test Mode override no longer exempts the Whirlpool's vertical-connection block (SC-Test-7) — is pinned by kit-descents.test.ts, with the web-side direction-picker fix pinned by apps/web's TestControlsPanel.test.tsx). Keep it that way — when a requirement changes, update both its `test` reference here and the test itself (see the repo `CLAUDE.md`).

# Appendix D — Cross-implementation conformance vectors

`docs/specs/conformance/` holds fixed solo playthroughs rendered as plain 7-bit-ASCII text —
everything a **foreign implementation** (e.g. the VAX Macro-32 port) needs to prove it reproduces
this engine: the initial `seed`/`picks`, the ordered action log to drive its reducer, a
post-action checkpoint per move (turn / level / area / phase / gs / **RNG cursor**), and the full
end state (party, map, working sets, score). The RNG cursor is the sharpest diagnostic: any
divergence in roll count, order, or LCG arithmetic (A.5) shows on the exact line it occurs.

- **Format & action grammar:** `docs/specs/conformance/README.md`.
- **Generator & drift guard:** `conformance-vectors.test.ts` — the vectors are vitest file
  snapshots, so the suite FAILS if engine behaviour drifts from what is committed; regenerate
  deliberately with `pnpm --filter engine exec vitest run -u src/conformance-vectors.test.ts`
  in the same commit as the approved rules change (mirror of the solo-golden discipline).
- **Coverage:** twenty runs. Eight mirror `solo-golden.test.ts` (combat, pickup, hazards,
  deaths); eleven base-game TARGETED fixtures are found by seed sweep, driven by steered policies
  committed in the generator: an escape with a laden party (`-escape`), a Sorcerer kill
  (`-sorcerer`), four artifact runs (`-artifacts`) that together use every usable artifact (Carpet 4,
  Lotus 5, Balm 6, Potion 8, Staff 9, Flute 12) and exercise the Lost-Ruby wrestle and the Treasure
  Chest, and five gap-coverage runs (`-chest2`, `-ring`, `-reclaim`, `-medusaavert`, `-petrified` —
  the last also pins the medusa pause: `MDS` phase, the untargeted `USE 5` throw,
  `medusaSlept`/`medusaAsleep`). The twentieth (`-kit`, Task 17) is the extension kit's own vector —
  the ONE `KIT 1` (`variants.extensionKit: true`) run, seed 165018/party 18,20 (originally seed 397,
  re-swept 2026-08-02 when the Chasm stopped drawing a small-pack card on arrival), driven by a "kit"
  policy that ports `kit-golden.test.ts`'s own decision logic verbatim so the two fixtures replay the
  identical action sequence; it pins Chasm/Whirlpool/Well specials and a parked Crypt (SC-EXT-5/6/7/
  13), all four kit hazards (SC-EXT-14/15/16/28), a kit `USE` — the Scroll (SC-EXT-25; Holy Water's own
  band is pinned separately in `kit-holywater-scroll.test.ts`) — and kit-creature reaction groups,
  ending in a natural `GS 2` party wipe (not a step-cap truncation). They are conformance smoke tests; the normative contract remains Part I.
- **Porting workflow:** `docs/specs/PORTING-GUIDE.md` ties Part I/II, Appendix A, and these
  vectors into a step-by-step plan for a port.
